# Full primitive certificates: cut slack and quantitative prefix control

Status: complete joint proof DRAFT, UNVERIFIED. This is max4's final
authorized round23. Fact04219554e41d9750 supplies the precise full seed
interface and its conditional asymptotic consequence, but does not verify
the new statements below. No seed existence or nonexistence is claimed.
No optimizer, numerical evaluation, finite replay, or fact_submit is used.

Novelty relative to the shared record: f7cee3034df8cb7f already derived the
exact saturated-cut structure and the signed tail credit. The new content
here is an exact positive-slack budget, including steps below1, its full
matrix consequences, and explicit quantitative control of a prefix when
a cut is near equality. The equality case is rederived as a consistency
check; the old claim of automatic nonnegative tail restriction is not used.
All new implications are proved together here; no unverified finding is a
premise. The accepted inputs are04219554e41d9750 and1252b8823bb5a290.

## 1. Full formal interface and the gradient-difference matrix

Let r>=1, h_0,...,h_(r-1)>=0, P_i=sum_(t<i)h_t for0<=i<=r,
s=P_r, and C=1+s. Set h_r=0 only for formulas. Indices of the formal
gradients range over0,...,r. Let e be the all-ones column, and define

    T_ii=1, T_it=h_t for t<i, T_it=0 for t>i,
    omega=(-2h_0,...,-2h_(r-1),2s)^T,
    D=diag(h_0(2-h_0),...,h_(r-1)(2-h_(r-1)),-s(s+2)),
    L=diag(Lambda e)-Lambda,  M=D+(LT+T^T L^T)/2.

Assume Lambda_ii=0, all off-diagonal Lambda_ij>=0, L^T e=omega,
and M>=0 on the entire original space R^(r+1). Thus this is precisely
the full finite certificate in fact04219554e41d9750, with arbitrary r.
No multiplier support, rank, or equality between formal gradients is imposed.

For x_i=x_0-sum_(t<i)h_t g_t, define

    Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2,
    V_h=2sum_(i<r)h_i(f_i-f_r)+sum_(i<r)h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2
                                               -s(s+1)||g_r||^2.

The full coefficient identity from fact04219554e41d9750 is

    V_h+sum_(i!=j)Lambda_ij Q_ij = <M,Gram(g_0,...,g_r)>.       (1)

The residuals Q_ij are nonpositive on every actual convex differentiable
1-smooth trajectory by fact1252b8823bb5a290. Hence this interface implies
V_h>=0 on all such trajectories with a minimizer.

Here is an algebraic proof of the constant-gradient kernel, needed below.
Since Te=e+(P_0,...,P_r)^T and
2sum_(i<r)h_i P_i=s^2-sum_(i<r)h_i^2, we have

    e^T D e=-s^2-sum h_i^2,
    e^T LT e=omega^T Te=s^2+sum h_i^2.

Thus e^T M e=0. Positive semidefiniteness implies Me=0: otherwise for
some y the linear term in (e+t y)^T M(e+t y) would be negative for one
sign of sufficiently small t.

For0<=j<r let u^j be the indicator of indices0,...,j. Let U have these
r columns and put K_jump=U^T M U. For any scalar formal vector g,

    g=e*g_r+sum_(j<r)u^j(g_j-g_(j+1)),
    g^T M g=sum_(i,j<r)(K_jump)_ij(g_i-g_(i+1))(g_j-g_(j+1)). (2)

In particular K_jump>=0. Conversely, under Me=0, its PSD property is
equivalent to full M>=0 by(2). This is a congruence identity retaining
all formal gradients, not a deletion of zero-step coordinates.

## 2. A legal cut trajectory for every positive step

Fix j with b=h_j>0. In this section define

    P=P_j, R=s-P_(j+1), A=1+P, B=1+R, d=b-1,
    v=1-b                 if 0<b<=1,
    v=(1-b)/B             if b>=1,
    w=1-v>0.

The two formulas agree at b=1. There is one legal scalar convex1-smooth
function with a minimizer whose GD trajectory has gradients1 at all
indices i<=j and v at all indices k>j, starting from x_0=A. Its positions
are

    x_i=1+P_j-P_i                         (i<=j),
    x_k=1-b-v(P_k-P_(j+1))                (k>j).             (3)

All sampled values can be chosen as f_i=x_i g_i-g_i^2/2.

For b>1, use the derivative clip(x,[v,1]) and f(0)=0. The bridge lands
at -d=vB, the suffix remains at or below v, and its terminal point is v.
The derivative is continuous, nondecreasing and1-Lipschitz, and0 is a
minimizer. On each plateau the stated values follow by integration.

For0<b<=1, v=1-b>=0. Put zeta=v(1-R)-1, strictly below every suffix
sample and below v. Define a derivative on the whole real line by

    f'(x)=0                  for x<=zeta-v,
          x-zeta+v           for zeta-v<=x<=zeta,
          v                  for zeta<=x<=v,
          x                  for v<=x<=1,
          1                  for x>=1.

Choose the integration constant by f(v)=v^2/2. This derivative is again
continuous, nondecreasing and1-Lipschitz, and its zero region contains
minimizers. The bridge lands at v. The suffix stays in[zeta,v], so its
gradient is v. Integration gives the stated sampled values on both
plateaus. Degenerate intervals when v=0 cause no difficulty. The minimum
value need not be zero; V_h and every finite Q are invariant under adding
a constant to f.

For any scalar data satisfying f_i=x_i g_i-g_i^2/2, telescoping the GD
squares gives

    V_h=x_0^2-(x_r+s g_r)^2.

On(3), x_r=-d-vR, hence the exact energy is

    E_j=A^2-[(A+d)v-d]^2.                                (4)

The same sampled-value identity gives Q_ik=(g_i-g_k)(x_k-g_k).
Define, for k>j,

    t_jk=d+v(1+P_k-P_(j+1)).

These numbers are nonnegative, with the more transparent forms

    t_jk=v(P_k-P_(j+1))          if0<b<=1,
    t_jk=(-v)(s-P_k)             ifb>=1.

Every within-block residual is zero. Every cross residual is exactly

    -Q_(i,k)=w*t_jk,
    -Q_(k,i)=w*(P_j-P_i),              i<=j<k.             (5)

Since g=v e+w u^j and Me=0, substitution into the complete identity(1)
gives the exact slack budget

    E_j=w^2(K_jump)_jj
       +w sum_(i<=j<k)[t_jk Lambda_(i,k)
                                      +(P_j-P_i)Lambda_(k,i)]. (6)

Every term on the right is nonnegative. Equation(6) keeps every original
cross multiplier, including both directions and both endpoints.

## 3. Step-only matrix bounds and a quadratic compatibility test

For each positive h_j define beta_j=E_j/w^2 using the preceding formulas.
A feasible full certificate necessarily has beta_j>=0 and

    0<=(K_jump)_jj<=beta_j.                              (7)

When b>=1, the expression simplifies to

    beta_j=[A^2 B^2-C^2 d^2]/(B+d)^2,
    C=A+B+d.                                             (8)

For0<b<=1 it is

    beta_j=[A^2-(1-b)^2(A+b)^2]/b^2.                     (9)

No negative-v trajectory is used in deriving(9). In particular the
small-step case is not obtained by applying a wrong-sign Huber residual.

For this section suppose all steps are positive. Set beta_(-1)=beta_r=0
and Drow_i=sqrt(beta_(i-1))+sqrt(beta_i) for0<=i<=r.
For a PSD matrix, its2-by-2 principal minors give
|(K_jump)_ik|<=sqrt(beta_i beta_k). Equation(2), applied to a standard
coordinate vector, therefore gives

    M_ii<=Drow_i^2,
    |(M u^j)_i|<=Drow_i sqrt(beta_j).                    (10)

The second inequality is the PSD Cauchy--Schwarz inequality; it also
follows by requiring (e_i+t u^j)^T M(e_i+t u^j)>=0 for every real t.
For every real vector z of length r,

    0<=z^T K_jump z<=(sum_(i<r)|z_i|sqrt(beta_i))^2.      (11)

An additional necessary condition involves no unknown multipliers.
Put p_0=1, p_(i+1)=(1-h_i)p_i. The actual quadratic f(x)=x^2/2,
started at1, has x_i=g_i=p_i and every finite Q_ik=0. Consequently

    V_h=1-C^2 p_r^2,
    0<=1-C^2 p_r^2
          <=(sum_(i<r)|h_i p_i|sqrt(beta_i))^2.           (12)

The upper inequality follows from(2),(11), since p_i-p_(i+1)=h_i p_i.
Equation(12) is only a necessary compatibility test. Neither it nor the
scalar inequalities beta_j>=0 are asserted sufficient.

## 4. Explicit stability at a cut with b>1

Assume all h_i>0 and fix a cut j with b=h_j>1. Continue using the local
notation P,R,A,B,d above, and set

    a=d/B, w=1+a, delta=beta_j,
    m=min_(i<r)h_i>0, Delta_cut=AB-Cd.

Equation(8) implies Delta_cut>=0 and d<B: if d>=B, then Cd>=CB>AB.
Write u=Lambda_(j,r), v_k=Lambda_(k,j) for k>j, and set
c_k=h_k for j<k<r, c_r=1; thus sum_(k>j)c_k=B.

The ideal cross array has only the entries

    Lambda^0_(j,r)=d q, Lambda^0_(k,j)=q c_k (k>j),
    q=2(A+d)/(B-d),                                     (13)

with every other cross entry zero. At exact cut equality this is the
usual primitive-join array. Away from equality it is just an explicitly
defined comparison array; it is not claimed to be a certificate.

Here are step-only error bounds, with empty sums interpreted as zero:

    Fbd=w delta/(a m) if j<r-1, and Fbd=0 otherwise,
    Bbd=w delta/m     if j>0,   and Bbd=0 otherwise,
    Ubd=[2 sqrt(delta)sum_(i<j)Drow_i+P Bbd]/B,
    eta=Fbd+Bbd+Ubd,
    Ebd=2 sqrt(delta)sum_(k>j)Drow_k+2C(r-j)eta,
    Lbd=[2B/(B-d)] [eta+Ebd/d].                         (14)

Then the sum of the absolute differences of ALL crossing coefficients
in the supplied full array and(13) is at most Lbd:

    sum_(i<=j<k)(|Lambda_(i,k)-Lambda^0_(i,k)|
                      +|Lambda_(k,i)-Lambda^0_(k,i)|)<=Lbd. (15)

Here is a derivation, keeping track of the off-support terms.
First (6) with t_jk=a(s-P_k) yields

    Factual=sum_(i<=j,j<k<r)Lambda_(i,k)<=Fbd,
    Bactual=sum_(k>j,i<j)Lambda_(k,i)<=Bbd.              (16)

The weights s-P_k and P_j-P_i in these sums are at least m. The
remaining potentially unwanted cross terms are u_i=Lambda_(i,r),i<j.
For i<j, summing the complete mixed matrix entries gives

    2(M 1_right)_i
      =-B u_i-sum_(j<l<r)(1+P_l-P_(j+1))Lambda_(i,l)
       -sum_(k>j)Lambda_(k,i)
       +h_i sum_(k>j,l<=i)Lambda_(k,l).                 (17)

In(17), 1_right=e-u^j. By Me=0 and(10), the absolute value of its left
side is at most2Drow_i sqrt(delta). The two subtracted sums are
nonnegative. Thus

    B u_i<=2Drow_i sqrt(delta)+h_i Bactual.

Summing gives Uactual=sum_(i<j)u_i<=Ubd. Hence the total mass of all
cross entries absent from(13) is at most eta.

For completeness, the full off-diagonal formula for i<k is

    2M_ik=-(Lambda_ik+Lambda_ki)
          -h_k sum_(l>k)Lambda_il+h_i sum_(l<=i)Lambda_kl. (18)

It follows directly by multiplying L and T; set h_r=0 in this formula.
For fixed k>j, summing absolute values in(18) over i<j gives

    sum_(i<j)|2M_ik|
       <=(1+h_k)(Factual+Uactual)+(1+P)Bactual<=C eta.

Since (M u^j)_k=M_kj+sum_(i<j)M_ki, equation(10) gives
|2M_jk|<=2Drow_k sqrt(delta)+C eta.
Formula(18) at i=j gives, for k<r,

    2M_jk=d v_k-h_k u
        -Lambda_jk-h_k sum_(k<l<r)Lambda_jl
                                  +b sum_(l<j)Lambda_kl,

and at k=r gives 2M_jr=d v_r-u+b sum_(l<j)Lambda_rl.
The terms after d v_k-c_k u have total absolute value at most C eta.
Therefore, for every k>j,

    |d v_k-c_k u|<=e_k,
    e_k=2Drow_k sqrt(delta)+2C eta,
    sum_(k>j)e_k=Ebd.                                  (19)

Sum the original value-flow equations over the left block. Its outward
flow is u+Uactual+Factual and its inward flow is sum v_k+Bactual. Thus

    |sum_(k>j)v_k-u-2(A+d)|<=eta.

Together with(19) this implies

    |u-d q|<=[d eta+Ebd]/(B-d)=:Uerr,
    |v_k-q c_k|<=[c_k Uerr+e_k]/d.

Adding these bounds and the off-support mass eta gives

    eta+(1+B/d)Uerr+Ebd/d
        =[2B/(B-d)] [eta+Ebd/d]=Lbd,

which proves(15). In particular this controls the entire cross array,
not a selected sparse ansatz. No assertion about the internal arrays'
signs after tail restriction has entered the calculation.

## 5. Quantitative primitive-energy bound for the actual prefix

Let h_left=(h_0,...,h_(j-1)), of sum P and capacity A. Under the
hypotheses of Section4, EVERY actual convex1-smooth trajectory of this
prefix obeys

    V_(h_left)>=-[Rbd+(2P+b+1/2)Lbd] Gmax^2,             (20)
    Rbd=(A+d)Delta_cut/(B-d),
    Gmax=max_(0<=i<=j)||g_i||.

The energy of an empty prefix is0. For a nonempty prefix, no initial
distance normalization or bounded minimizer distance is assumed in(20).

To prove the claim, take any actual prefix data. Translate positions
and shift values so x_j=0 and f_j=0. Append formal tail data by setting
every g_k=f_k=0 for k>j and x_k=-b g_j for k>j. These tail data are NOT
asserted to arise from an actual function. They are permitted for
evaluating the full formal PSD identity(1). All tail-internal Q vanish.

Using the ideal cross array(13), direct substitution yields

    V_h+sum_cross Lambda^0_ik Q_ik
                      =V_(h_left)+Rbd||g_j||^2.         (21)

For an explicit coefficient check before the f_j=0 shift, V_h-V_left
has value coefficient2(P+b)f_j and gradient coefficient
b(1-b)+P(P+1) on ||g_j||^2. The ideal cross sum contributes
q(d-B)f_j+q[B/2-d(b-1/2)]||g_j||^2. The value coefficients cancel
because q(B-d)=2(A+d)=2(P+b). The remaining coefficient is

    A^2-(1+q)d^2
      =[(A+d)(AB-d(A+B+d))]/(B-d)=Rbd.

The actual prefix inequalities Q_ik<=0 and Lambda_ik>=0 make their
contribution in(1) nonpositive. Every cross residual on these formal
data has absolute value at most(2P+b+1/2)Gmax^2: actual convexity gives
|f_i-f_j|<=Gmax||x_i-x_j||<=P Gmax^2, GD gives
||x_i-x_j||<=P Gmax, and the formulas are

    Q_(i,k)=f_i+<g_i,-b g_j-x_i>+||g_i||^2/2,
    Q_(k,i)=-f_i+||g_i||^2/2,                    i<=j<k.

Equations(1),(15),(21) and full M>=0 now give(20).
This proof uses the unobserved formal extension only inside a PSD
identity; it never claims those data constitute a legal GD witness.

The error Rbd is linear in the scalar cut gap:

    delta=Delta_cut(AB+Cd)/(B+d)^2,
    Rbd=delta (A+d)(B+d)^2/[(B-d)(AB+Cd)].              (22)

Consequently, along a bounded positive-step family with m and d bounded
away from0, B-d bounded away from0, and all beta_i bounded, the right
side's coefficient in(20) is O(sqrt(delta)). This asymptotic notation
is only a consequence of the explicit bounds(14),(22).

An actual prefix witness with V_left=-nu Gmax^2, nu>0, therefore forces

    nu<=Rbd+(2P+b+1/2)Lbd.                              (23)

This is an explicit necessary condition on the parent steps. It supplies
a quantitative route from a prefix obstruction to a positive gap at the
parent cut, rather than assuming an exactly saturated cut.

For a concrete symbolic instance, take a parent whose first step is3/2
and select j=1, assuming its selected bridge h_1>1 and all steps positive.
For its one-step prefix, f(x)=x^2/2 started at1 has gradients1,-1/2,
Gmax=1 and V_left=1-(5/2)^2(1/2)^2=-9/16. Therefore every such parent
full certificate must satisfy

    Rbd+(b+7/2)Lbd>=9/16.                              (24)

In any bounded family meeting the positivity conditions after(22),
this prohibits approaching delta=0 with that fixed first step. It does
not exclude a parent with a positive cut gap or any unrelated seed.

## 6. Equality, zero steps, and exact symbolic checks

At delta=0, every quantity in(14) vanishes and Delta_cut=0. Thus all
cross entries are exactly(13). Their mixed gradient block is zero by
(18),(19). The left internal multipliers are nonnegative and give a
full certificate for V_left, since(21) now has Rbd=0 and the full left
matrix is the corresponding principal PSD block. This recovers the
old saturated-prefix restriction by a new limiting-compatible estimate.

Writing kappa=(C/B)^2 at equality gives q=kappa-1. The accepted full
primitive-join identity then shows that the tail has a certificate for

    V_tail-(1-1/kappa)sum_(k<r)h_k Q_(k,r),

where this sum is over nonterminal TAIL indices. Removing this signed
credit would subtract q h_k from its actual coefficient Lambda_(k,r).
Nothing in the preceding estimates asserts that those differences are
nonnegative. Thus no automatic ordinary primitive-tail restriction is
inferred; the old counterexample remains relevant.

Zero steps require care. The formulas(1),(2),(6) at a POSITIVE cut hold
even if other steps vanish. But(7)--(15) for every coordinate are not
claimed for an original word containing zeros. A full certificate can
have arbitrarily large curvature in an unused zero-step gradient direction.
For example h=(0), Lambda_01=Lambda_10=t>=0 satisfies the complete
flow equations and has M=t[[1,-1],[-1,1]]. Its jump matrix is[t], with
no finite step-only bound on t. This is an exact counterexample to
extending beta=2-b^2 continuously to a formal coordinate at b=0.

For necessary schedule tests one may instead compress zero steps in a
justified way. Retain the original index of each positive step and the
original terminal index. Identify formal jets within each repeated-query
group only to RESTRICT a given full certificate. The GD formulas and
V_h become exactly those of the positive-step compressed word. An edge
within one group vanishes after restriction; every other edge becomes
the corresponding compressed Q with its nonnegative coefficients summed.
Thus a full original certificate implies a full compressed certificate.

Conversely, a full compressed primitive certificate embeds into the
original formal space by assigning its edges only to the retained indices
and assigning zero to other edges. Zero steps contribute nothing to V_h
or to any queried displacement, and the original terminal is retained.
Therefore the embedded identity is the pullback of the compressed PSD
matrix and is PSD on ALL original independent gradients. No equality
between zero-step gradients is needed for this converse. The all-zero
word has the zero certificate. This proves feasibility equivalence while
also explaining why the original matrix entries need not be bounded.

In particular the tests above may be applied to the compressed word,
and the exact saturated-prefix conclusion may be lifted to the original
prefix by this embedding. The quantitative constants for a compressed
word refer to its compressed coefficient array, not an unjustified bound
on every possible original zero-step coefficient.

For one positive step b, the only value-flow and constant-kernel solution
is Lambda_01=2, Lambda_10=2b+2. Direct expansion gives

    V_(b)+2Q_01+(2b+2)Q_10=(2-b^2)||g_0-g_1||^2.

Both versions(8),(9) reduce exactly to beta_0=2-b^2. Thus they recover
the complete one-step interface and its endpoint b=sqrt(2), including
the b<1 branch, without a numerical evaluation.

## 7. Scope and remaining missing object

For the C78 problem set r=4 and require sum h_i>=168/25, retaining all20
original nonnegative multipliers and the full5-gradient matrix. The new
constraints(6),(12),(15),(20) are necessary for that complete interface.
They complement scalar cut bounds and can support exact rejection or
reconstruction of a proposed seed. No candidate word has been evaluated
numerically here; no witness with the required capacity has been found,
and no separator for the entire feasible set has been established.

The original target remains open. The sole accepted scalar/composition
bridge is fact04219554e41d9750. This draft has no verifier allocation and
may not be used as a fact. The final-cut equality subgoal in the round23
plan was already present in shared work; the positive-slack estimates,
small-step trajectories and zero-coordinate warning are the additions.

Justification: explicit actual-function construction, coefficient
identities and elementary PSD inequalities. Main fragility checks are
the direction of cross residuals, small-step plateau legality, the full
off-diagonal signs, and the distinction between a formal extension and
an actual trajectory. Each is expanded above. Confidence before independent
verification:0.97; no verifier acceptance is claimed.

证据标签：source_document_no_new_verification。以下内容原样收录，不因汇编而获得新的证明地位。

<!-- BEGIN_SOURCE_BODY DOC-003228 -->
Definitions from accepted b98bb807d514e80d: P_n=S_circle(n)+1, B_n=2S_bullet(n)+1, P0=B0=1. Their exact recursions are P_n=max_(k+l=n-1)F(P_k,P_l), B_n=max_(k+l=n-1)G(P_k,B_l), with F(x,y)=(x+y+sqrt((x+y)^2+4xy))/2 and G(x,y)=2x+y/2+sqrt(y^2+8xy)/2.

SCALAR COMPARISON. Set alpha=2lambda-1=lambda(4-lambda)>0, L=(4x+lambda^2 y)/alpha. Its coefficients exceed1, so L>=x+y. Exact expansion gives alpha^2[L^2-(x+y)L-xy]=(2x/lambda-sqrt2 lambda y)^2>=0. Thus L>=F(x,y), the positive root of z^2-(x+y)z-xy=0. At z=lambda F(x,y), the polynomial defining G(x,lambda y) evaluates to z^2-(4x+lambda y)z+4x^2=x[4x+lambda^2 y-alpha F(x,y)]>=0. Also z>=lambda(x+y)>=(4x+lambda y)/2, so it lies above the larger root G. Equality holds for x=0 and for x/y=lambda^2/sqrt2 with y>0. Homogeneity gives G(x,c y)/c=G(x/c,y), strictly decreasing in c for x>0, so any c<lambda fails at that equality point. No squaring-sign condition was omitted.

DP INDUCTION. G is nondecreasing in its second argument. If B_l<=lambda P_l, then G(P_k,B_l)<=G(P_k,lambda P_l)<=lambda F(P_k,P_l). Taking maxima proves B_n<=lambda P_n from the base.

DYADIC PRIMITIVE BOUND, NO PROFILE LIMIT. Let rho=log_2lambda>1. For t>0 put D(t)=(1+t)^rho-1 and g(z)=log D(exp z). With t=exp z, differentiation gives g''(z)=rho t(1+t)^(rho-2)[(1+t)^rho-1-rho t]/D(t)^2>0. The bracket is positive by its derivative rho[(1+t)^(rho-1)-1]>0 and its zero value at0. Consequently D(t)D(1/t)>=D(1)^2=(lambda-1)^2=2. For x,y>0 put A=x^(1/rho),B=y^(1/rho),T=(A+B)^rho. This inequality gives (T-x)(T-y)>=2xy, so T^2-(x+y)T-xy>=0; T>=x+y by superadditivity of the power, and hence F(x,y)<=T. Zero arguments follow directly. Induction now gives P_n<=(n+1)^rho. Balanced joins give P_(2^m-1)>=lambda^m, hence equality. Therefore B_(2^m-1)<=lambda^(m+1) at every m.

ACTUAL NORMALIZATION. For c_m=lambda(1+1/m),a_m=(1-1/m)/c_m and H_(j+1)=(H_j,1+a_m c_m^j,H_j), M_j=1+sum H_j satisfies M_(j+1)=2M_j+a_m c_m^j. Thus M_m=A_m c_m^m+(1-A_m)2^m, A_m=a_m/(c_m-2)->1, and M_m/lambda^m->e. Define explicitly K_Hub,m=1/(2M_m-1), without presuming that this equals every earlier K notation. Then limsup B K_Hub<=lambda/(2e). If a genuine full certificate supplies q_m<=C K_Hub,m+o(K_Hub,m) for any C<2e/lambda, then limsup q_m B<=C lambda/(2e)<1. Equivalently q_m M_m has limsup<e/lambda as a sufficient condition. In particular q_m<=1/M_m would give limsup q_m B<=lambda/e<1; lambda<5/2<e. This is a sufficient scalar threshold only, not an upper certificate. The same proof yields thresholds 2exp(tau)/lambda and exp(tau)/lambda for the fixed deformation c_m=lambda(1+tau/m),a_m=(1-sigma/m)/c_m. All new statements remain unverified candidates; accepted b98 provides definitions, and all new inequalities are proved here rather than using unverified findings as premises.

# Original join-row redistribution: two explicit full-space H4 obstructions

JOINT UNVERIFIED. Worker max2, invocation84, ended baseline83.
Project gd-exact-profile-20260926; guidance b9c4a86f186c5b26.
One invocation, completed ceiling84/attempted85, controller cap1.
No formal call or fact_submit is authorized. Everything new below is draft
mathematics, not an accepted fact or a downstream correctness premise.

## 1. Outcome and exact scope

The prescribed common-zeta family is excluded for EVERY admissible zeta
and EVERY remaining attachment tuple. Literal zeta=1 is strictly inside its
allowed row-weight interval. It cancels the old inner defect at zero
terminal transfer, but has a different full-space negative direction.
The failure is not inferred from a single negative matrix coefficient.

The two cases are complementary. If zeta<1, the source's full block-constant
direction remains strictly negative. If zeta>=1, varying g3,g11,rL,rR
together about the common-gradient contact point gives an explicitly
negative value of the entire nineteen-coordinate quadratic. The new
contact equations independently expose the incompatible requirement
zeta<=2k/(C+k)<1 for PSD. They are derived afresh, including all returned rows.

Only after this first result, one second mechanism was frozen in shared
plan1618a1631e5cd3f0: a signed redistribution along two ORIGINAL directed
join triangles. It removes the newly exposed excess contact at g3,g11.
All its final original row weights are nonnegative, including the outer
row overlaps. Its complete remainder is nonetheless strictly negative on
the block-constant witness, now with exact unpaid amount
(1+kapO)d_i(C-k)/(C+k), before the further terminal-transfer square.
A second, explicitly evaluated one-coordinate perturbation confirms the
contact has moved to g4 rather than disappeared. No third family is opened.

These are obstructions to two specified sufficient conic/PSD certificates.
Neither witness is asserted to be a legal projection history. The actual
H4 endpoint inequality is not refuted. Both mandatory actual histories
are retained with the complete actual right H3 and all remainders; their
full endpoint gaps are positive. No child is reset.

The accepted predecessor is fact1e0bf3f41457d8e4. Its Part B proves the
actual H3 U law for every d>=1 and supplies exact H2/off-root/remainder
identities. Its universality theorem is not reproved, and H3 is not used
as if it were H4. No sufficient H4 certificate or recursive interface is
obtained here. H4 at d=1, H4 at all d, all-depth propagation, and the
original asymptotic minimax target remain OPEN. The original selected
minimizer denominator and W=2R are unchanged.

## 2. Fixed original word and all actual samples

Fix ONE integer m>=1536 throughout. Define

 epsilon=1/(4m), beta=1-epsilon, lambda=1+sqrt(2),
 c=lambda(1+epsilon), a=beta/c, d_j=a*c^j,
 H0=(), H_(j+1)=(H_j,1+d_j,H_j), S_j=sum H_j.

Use these abbreviations, retaining the exact meanings in source finding
11adbf577f491d4a:

 A=1+a, B=1+beta, t=S2=2A+B, C=1+t,
 k=beta*c=d_2, K=beta*c^2=d_3, ell=C+k,
 s=S3=2C+k-1=t+ell, C3=1+s=2C+k,
 b=1+K, h=s+b=C3+K, S=S4=2s+b.

S is the LOCAL mass of H4, not the enclosing S_m. Its fifteen steps are

 (h0,...,h14)=(A,B,A,1+k,A,B,A,1+K,A,B,A,1+k,A,B,A).    (2.1)

For a fixed nonempty compact convex set D0 containing zero in a positive
finite-dimensional real Euclidean space, write P for projection onto D0,

 F(X)=max_(g in D0)(<X,g>-||g||^2/2),
 U_d(X)=max_(g in D0)(2<X,g>-d||g||^2), d>0.

Every physical query obeys x_(i+1)=x_i-h_i*g_i, g_i=P(x_i), i<15.
The actual terminal gradient is g15=P(x15); it supplies no GD step. Put

 x=x0, u=x3, z=x4, v=x7, w=x8, uR=x11, zR=x12, y=x15.

Thus z=u-(1+k)g3, w=v-b*g7, zR=uR-(1+k)g11. At d=1 set

 J=1+2t, D=1+2s, M=1+2S=D+2h,
 eta=M-2s=D+2b, E=eta+2t,
 M=E+2ell, D=J+2ell, E-J=2h.                          (2.2)

The auxiliary samples on the SAME D0 are ACTUAL maximizers

 p0=P(x/M), rL=P(z/E), rR=P(zR/J), q=P(y)=g15,
 qL=P(v/eta), rO=P(w/D).                              (2.3)

Their sample inputs are respectively x/M,z/E,zR/J,y,v/eta,w/D.
No extra physical steps or arbitrary feasible replacements are introduced.
For any sample labels i,j use the original ordered orientation

 Q_(i,j)=<X_j-G_j,G_i-G_j><=0.                        (2.4)

This is the projection specialization in accepted1e0bf3f41457d8e4,
Part B(2.3), citing its predecessor1252b8823bb5a290. A nonnegative row
coefficient below ALWAYS means a coefficient of -Q_(i,j).

## 3. Complete base rows, reserves, and endpoint identity

The following identities are jointly reconstructed from accepted Part B,
not assumed true because they appeared in an earlier unverified finding.
For a word of mass T, input X, endpoint Y, its primitive energy is
V=||X||^2-||Y+T*P(Y)||^2, by accepted Part B(2.4).

Set

 C1=1+A, kap1=(3C1+beta)/(C1-beta),
 R1=(C1+beta)[C1^2-beta(2C1+beta)]/(C1-beta),
 deltaA=2-A^2,
 e0=deltaA, e1=R1(2/B-1), e2=kap1*deltaA+R1(2/A-1).

The eight H2 multipliers from accepted Part B(3.5)-(3.6) are

 Lambda_01=2, Lambda_10=2(1+A), Lambda_12=2R1/B,
 Lambda_21=(kap1-1)A+2R1/B,
 Lambda_13=(kap1-1)beta, Lambda_31=kap1-1,
 Lambda_23=2kap1+(kap1-1)A+2R1/A,
 Lambda_32=2kap1(1+A)+2R1/A.                          (3.1)

All unlisted entries are zero. All listed entries and e0,e1,e2 are
positive, as proved in accepted Part B(3.4)-(3.7).
For o in {0,4,8,12} let

 C_o=-sum_(i!=j in {0,1,2,3})Lambda_ij Q_(o+i,o+j),
 P_o=sum_(j=0)^2 e_j||g_(o+j)-g_(o+j+1)||^2,
 V_o=C_o+P_o.

Use C00,C01,C10,C11 for offsets0,4,8,12, and analogous P,V labels. Set

 kap=(3C+k)/(C-k), kapO=(3C3+K)/(C3-K),
 d_i=(C+k)[k(2C+k)-C^2]/(C-k),
 d_o=(C3+K)[K(2C3+K)-C3^2]/(C3-K),
 Bterm=kapO*d_i+d_o, Di=(1+kapO)d_i.                  (3.2)

The defect d_i is not a sequence element d_j. Both d_i,d_o are strictly
positive. To check, put u0=sqrt(2)-1 and use

 k-u0*C=epsilon[u0+4u0^2/(1+epsilon)-lambda*epsilon]>0.

Here u0>2/5,lambda<5/2,epsilon<=1/6144. Also
K/C3=c*(k/C)/(2+k/C)>u0, since the displayed rational map is increasing,
c>lambda and lambda*u0/(2+u0)=u0. Factor
k(2C+k)-C^2=(k-u0*C)(k+lambda*C), and similarly for K,C3.
The elementary bounds C>11/2,k<5/2,C3>13,K<25/4 imply C>k,C3>K.
Thus all denominators and the stated defects are positive.

The three ORIGINAL join sums are

 E_i=sum_(j=4)^6 h_j Q_(j,3)+Q_(7,3)+k Q_(3,7)
                                      +sum_(j=4)^6 h_j Q_(j,7),
 E_r=sum_(j=12)^14 h_j Q_(j,11)+Q_(15,11)+k Q_(11,15)
                                      +sum_(j=12)^14 h_j Q_(j,15),
 E_o=sum_(j=8)^14 h_j Q_(j,7)+Q_(15,7)+K Q_(7,15)
                                      +sum_(j=8)^14 h_j Q_(j,15).

Define

 C_rows=C00+kap*C01+kapO*C10+kapO*kap*C11
       -(kap-1)E_i-kapO*(kap-1)E_r-(kapO-1)E_o,
 P_sq=P00+kap*P01+kapO*P10+kapO*kap*P11.              (3.3)

Here is the full base coefficient array, including every overlap. Begin
with zero at every ordered pair. For each o in{0,4,8,12}, add nu_o Lambda_ij
at pair(o+i,o+j), with

                 (nu_0,nu_4,nu_8,nu_12)=(1,kap,kapO,kapO*kap).

For each tuple (p,n,I,e,Lam) in

 (3,7,{4,5,6},k,kap-1),
 (11,15,{12,13,14},k,kapO*(kap-1)),
 (7,15,{8,...,14},K,kapO-1),

add Lam*h_j at(j,p) and(j,n) for each j in I, Lam at(n,p), and Lam*e
at(p,n). Sum coincident entries. Every base entry is now explicitly given.

Apply accepted Part B(3.3) to both inner joins with capacities C,C, then
to the outer join with capacities C3,C3. Its off-root coefficient is -d_i
or -d_o. Substitution gives the EXACT full primitive identity

 V4=C_rows+P_sq+Fdef,
 Fdef=-d_i||g3||^2+(d_i-d_o)||g7||^2
                      -kapO*d_i||g11||^2+Bterm||q||^2.   (3.4)

For clarity: the two child defects are
-d_i(||g3||^2-||g7||^2) and
-kapO*d_i(||g11||^2-||q||^2); the outer defect is
-d_o(||g7||^2-||q||^2). This verifies every diagonal in(3.4).
No primitive positivity of H3 or H4 was assumed.

Let

 Hvec=sum_(i<15)h_i*g_i=x-y,
 Vmix=Hvec+(1+S)q, Wstar=M*p0-Vmix,
 Nstar=2<x-M*p0,Wstar>, Qbase=||Wstar||^2+P_sq+Fdef.

The mixture Vmix/M has nonnegative weights summing to M. Applying accepted
Part B(4.2),(4.5) to the WHOLE H4 at d=1 gives

 M[U_M(x)-U1(y)]=C_rows+Nstar+Qbase.                  (3.5)

This is a full endpoint equality, with the whole actual right H3 included.
It does not drop the matched radial terms: it reassembles them through the
accepted exact primitive and maximization identities. At d=1 the terminal
radial term is zero because q=P(y), not by a sign estimate.

## 4. Complete retained attachment family and original added rows

Put

 TL=sum_(i<4)h_i*g_i=x-z, TR=sum_(i<12)h_i*g_i=x-zR,
 L01=A*g4+B*g5+A*g6, L10=A*g8+B*g9+A*g10,
 Udiff=(1+k)g3+L01+b*g7+L10=u-uR,
 Zdiff=L01+b*g7+L10+(1+k)g11=z-zR.

The allocated nonnegative normals are

 Ng=-Q_(11,3)-Q_(3,11)=<Udiff,g3-g11>-||g3-g11||^2,
 Na=-E Q_(rR,rL)-J Q_(rL,rR)
   =<Zdiff,rL-rR>-E||rL||^2-J||rR||^2+(E+J)<rL,rR>,
 Nc=-Q_(rR,11)-J Q_(11,rR)
   =k||g11||^2+(J-k)<g11,rR>-J||rR||^2.              (4.1)

Na uses UNEQUAL denominators E,J and cancels x exactly. Retain

 theta=d_i/k, omega=d_i, tau>=0,
 wL,wR>=0, t_i>=0 (0<=i<15),
 t_i+wL*1_(i=3)+wR*1_(i=11)<=h_i.                   (4.2)

The proofs of obstruction in fact hold for arbitrary nonnegative theta,
omega, but their literal source values remain fixed in the actual family.
Write

 DeltaA=wL(rL-g3)+wR(rR-g11),
 DeltaT=sum_(i<15)t_i(q-g_i), Tsum=sum_(i<15)t_i,
 Delta=DeltaA+DeltaT, W2=Wstar-Delta,
 mu_i=h_i-t_i-wL*1_(i=3)-wR*1_(i=11),
 mu_q=1+S+Tsum, mu_L=wL, mu_R=wR,
 N2=2<x-M*p0,W2>.

These weights are nonnegative and sum to M. The other extracted original
normals are

 AL=<z-E*rL,rL-g3>=-E Q_(3,rL),
 AR=<zR-J*rR,rR-g11>=-J Q_(11,rR),
 NT_i=<y-q,q-g_i>=-Q_(i,15).                         (4.3)

Starting with(3.5), replace Nstar by N2+2<x-M*p0,Delta>.
Subtract2wL AL,2wR AR and2sum t_i NT_i. Since z=x-TL,zR=x-TR,
y=x-Hvec, this cancels the ENTIRE x coefficient and gives

 Q2=Qbase
    +2wL<TL+E*rL-M*p0,rL-g3>
    +2wR<TR+J*rR-M*p0,rR-g11>
    +2<Hvec+q-M*p0,DeltaT>-omega*Ng-theta*Na-tau*Nc,

 M[U_M(x)-U1(y)]
  =C_rows+N2+2wL*AL+2wR*AR+2sum t_i*NT_i
                            +omega*Ng+theta*Na+tau*Nc+Q2. (4.4)

This rederives source Q2 from the accepted endpoint identities rather than
using its unverified PSD claims. Every physical and auxiliary coordinate
has been retained. In particular expansion about the changed mixture has
-||Delta||^2; changing the mixture does not simply change the square.

For both new mechanisms below, keep ALL these additional ordered rows:

 (i,p0):2M mu_i for i<15; (15,p0):2M mu_q;
 (rL,p0):2M wL; (rR,p0):2M wR;
 (3,rL):2wL E;
 (11,rR):J(2wR+tau); (rR,11):tau;
 (11,3):omega; (3,11):omega;
 (rR,rL):theta E; (rL,rR):theta J;
 (i,15):2t_i for i<15.                              (4.5)

Add these to the changed base arrays, summing coincident entries. All other
additional entries are zero. This gives every final ordered coefficient,
including initial factor M and auxiliary factors E,J.

## 5. Mechanism1: exact common-zeta return and its admissible interval

Define the three actual symmetric physical normals

 JL=-Q_(7,3)-Q_(3,7), JR=-Q_(15,11)-Q_(11,15),
 JO=-Q_(15,7)-Q_(7,15).                              (5.1)

Let the corresponding displacements be

 DL=x3-x7=(1+k)g3+sum_(j=4)^6 h_j*g_j,
 DR=x11-x15=(1+k)g11+sum_(j=12)^14 h_j*g_j,
 DO=x7-x15=sum_(j=7)^14 h_j*g_j.

Adding the two ORIGINAL orientations in each pair gives exactly

 JL=<DL,g3-g7>-||g3-g7||^2,
 JR=<DR,g11-q>-||g11-q||^2,
 JO=<DO,g7-q>-||g7-q||^2.                            (5.2)

Each is nonnegative on an actual projection history. For zeta>=0 freeze

 rhoL=zeta*d_i/k, rhoR=kapO*rhoL, rhoO=zeta*d_o/K,
 Rz=rhoL*JL+rhoR*JR+rhoO*JO,
 Cz=C_rows-Rz, Qz=Q2+Rz.                            (5.3)

Replacing C_rows,Q2 by Cz,Qz in(4.4) preserves the endpoint equality.
This really changes the original row allocation. It is not an additional
normal with a silently changed sign.

Here are BOTH ordered coefficients at every affected original pair:

 pair        coefficient in Cz before the additions(4.5)
 (7,3)       kap-1-rhoL
 (3,7)       k(kap-1)-rhoL
 (15,11)     kapO(kap-1)-rhoR
 (11,15)     kapO*k(kap-1)+(kapO-1)(1+k)-rhoR
 (15,7)      kapO-1-rhoO
 (7,15)      K(kapO-1)-rhoO.                        (5.4)

The term(kapO-1)(1+k) at(11,15) is the ORIGINAL OUTER sum's j=11
contribution; it cannot be omitted. No H2 base pair coincides with any
of these six pairs. Formula(3.3)'s full entry rule proves this rather
than presuming disjoint children. In the complete final list, add2t11
at(11,15) and2t7 at(7,15), as prescribed by(4.5); the other four displayed
pairs receive no attachment addition.

Since k>1,K>1, the reverse orientations (7,3),(15,11),(15,7) are the
limiting ones. The interval is EXACTLY

 0<=zeta<=zeta_max,
 zeta_max=min{k(kap-1)/d_i, K(kapO-1)/d_o}
         =min{2k/delta_i,2K/delta_o},
 delta_i=k(2C+k)-C^2, delta_o=K(2C3+K)-C3^2.         (5.5)

The forward right-inner overlap makes its bound weaker, not stronger.
Even allowing the terminal additions to support a reduced forward row
does not enlarge(5.5), because its three limiting reverse rows are unchanged.
Every other original row is untouched and nonnegative.

Literal zeta=1 is strictly allowed. Here is an analytic bound, with no
numerical evaluation. Put Cref=lambda^2=5+2u0 and C3ref=lambda^3=2Cref+lambda.
Then

 0<Cref-C=epsilon[1+4u0/(1+epsilon)]<3epsilon,
 k=lambda(1-epsilon^2), 0<lambda-k<3epsilon^2,
 0<C3ref-C3<7epsilon,
 Kref=lambda^2, 0<K-Kref=Kref(epsilon-epsilon^2-epsilon^3)<6epsilon.

At(Cref,lambda) and(C3ref,Kref) the corresponding delta is zero. Therefore

 0<delta_i
   =(k-lambda)(k+lambda+2C)+(Cref-C)(Cref+C-2lambda)
   <36epsilon<1<2k,

 0<delta_o
   =(K-Kref)(K+Kref+2C3)+(C3ref-C3)(C3ref+C3-2Kref)
   <6epsilon*43+7epsilon*30=468epsilon<1<2K.         (5.6)

We used Cref<6,C3ref<15,Kref<6,K<25/4 and epsilon<=1/6144. In particular
zeta_max>1. These are uniform symbolic inequalities at the original m;
no fitted interval or recurrence replay is involved.

## 6. Entire nineteen-coordinate matrix, rederived with returned rows

The independent formal labels are I={p,0,1,...,15,L,R}; p means p0,
L means rL,R means rR and15 means q. A formal assignment gives an arbitrary
Euclidean vector G_alpha to each of these NINETEEN labels. It is not an
assertion that these vectors form a legal projection history. Let e^alpha
be the unit coefficient column at label alpha, and define
sym(a,b)=(a*b^T+b*a^T)/2. These columns are coefficient bookkeeping only.

All columns needed to specify every matrix entry are

 Hc=sum_(i<15)h_i e^i,
 wc=M e^p-Hc-(1+S)e^15,
 tcL=sum_(i<4)h_i e^i, tcR=sum_(i<12)h_i e^i,
 acL=tcL+E e^L-M e^p, dcL=e^L-e^3,
 acR=tcR+J e^R-M e^p, dcR=e^R-e^11,
 bc=Hc+e^15-M e^p,
 dcT=sum_(i<15)t_i(e^15-e^i),
 uc=sum_(i=3)^10 h_i e^i, dcG=e^3-e^11,
 zc=sum_(i=4)^11 h_i e^i, dcA=e^L-e^R,
 vcL=sum_(i=3)^6 h_i e^i, jcL=e^3-e^7,
 vcR=sum_(i=11)^14 h_i e^i, jcR=e^11-e^15,
 vcO=sum_(i=7)^14 h_i e^i, jcO=e^7-e^15.             (6.1)

Set f_3=-d_i,f_7=d_i-d_o,f_11=-kapO*d_i,f_15=Bterm, and all other
f_alpha=0. Define

 Pmat=sum_(o in{0,4,8,12})nu_o sum_(j=0)^2
                e_j(e^(o+j)-e^(o+j+1))(e^(o+j)-e^(o+j+1))^T,
 Gmat=sym(uc,dcG)-dcG*dcG^T,
 Amat=sym(zc,dcA)-E e^L(e^L)^T-J e^R(e^R)^T
                                      +(E+J)sym(e^L,e^R),
 Cmat=k e^11(e^11)^T+(J-k)sym(e^11,e^R)-J e^R(e^R)^T,
 JLmat=sym(vcL,jcL)-jcL*jcL^T,
 JRmat=sym(vcR,jcR)-jcR*jcR^T,
 JOmat=sym(vcO,jcO)-jcO*jcO^T.                        (6.2)

Thus the full changed matrix is

 Qzmat=wc*wc^T+Pmat+diag(f)
       +2wL sym(acL,dcL)+2wR sym(acR,dcR)+2sym(bc,dcT)
       -omega*Gmat-theta*Amat-tau*Cmat
       +rhoL*JLmat+rhoR*JRmat+rhoO*JOmat.             (6.3)

For specificity, the coefficient at EVERY ordered pair alpha,beta in I is

 (Qzmat)_(alpha,beta)=wc_alpha*wc_beta
  +sum_o nu_o sum_(j=0)^2 e_j
    [1_(alpha=o+j)-1_(alpha=o+j+1)]
    [1_(beta=o+j)-1_(beta=o+j+1)]
  +f_alpha*1_(alpha=beta)
  +wL[(acL)_alpha*(dcL)_beta+(dcL)_alpha*(acL)_beta]
  +wR[(acR)_alpha*(dcR)_beta+(dcR)_alpha*(acR)_beta]
  +bc_alpha*(dcT)_beta+(dcT)_alpha*bc_beta
  -omega*[((uc)_alpha*(dcG)_beta+(dcG)_alpha*(uc)_beta)/2
                                        -(dcG)_alpha*(dcG)_beta]
  -theta*[((zc)_alpha*(dcA)_beta+(dcA)_alpha*(zc)_beta)/2
           -E*1_(alpha=L)*1_(beta=L)-J*1_(alpha=R)*1_(beta=R)
           +(E+J)(1_(alpha=L)*1_(beta=R)
                         +1_(alpha=R)*1_(beta=L))/2]
  -tau*[k*1_(alpha=11)*1_(beta=11)
         +(J-k)(1_(alpha=11)*1_(beta=R)
                         +1_(alpha=R)*1_(beta=11))/2
         -J*1_(alpha=R)*1_(beta=R)]
  +sum_(a in{L,R,O})rho_a*
       [((vc_a)_alpha*(jc_a)_beta+(jc_a)_alpha*(vc_a)_beta)/2
                                       -(jc_a)_alpha*(jc_a)_beta]. (6.4)

In the last line use precisely(vcL,jcL),(vcR,jcR),(vcO,jcO) and
rhoL,rhoR,rhoO. Equation(2.1) gives all physical h_i; no matrix coefficient
is an unspecified optimization variable. Norm coefficients are diagonal
entries; mixed inner-product coefficients are twice the corresponding
entry. Appending x as a twentieth coordinate gives a ZERO row and column:
(4.4) canceled its coefficient, and each returned symmetric pair(5.2)
also cancels x exactly. No quotient or identification has reduced the
formal space.

## 7. New common-gradient contact equations

Let all nineteen coordinates equal a common nonzero G. Every term of Qz
vanishes, so Qz=0. A PSD quadratic must have zero first variation there:
otherwise either sign of a sufficiently small perturbation makes it
negative. This follows directly by expanding the quadratic in that scalar
perturbation. We now differentiate(4.4),(5.2) with the NEW rows included.

At this point TL=ell G,TR=(h+ell)G,Udiff=Zdiff=hG,
DL=DR=ell G,DO=hG. The linear coefficients from the returned rows are

 rhoL*ell at g3, -rhoL*ell+rhoO*h at g7,
 rhoR*ell at g11, -rhoR*ell-rhoO*h at q.              (7.1)

There are no other first-order join-return coefficients. The entire
contact system is consequently

 2ell*wL=theta*h,
 2(h+ell)wR=tau*(J+k)-theta*h,
 t_i=0 for i not in{3,7,11},
 S*t3=d_i+(omega-theta)h/2-rhoL*ell/2,
 S*t7=d_o-d_i+rhoL*ell/2-rhoO*h/2,
 S*t11=kapO*d_i+(theta-omega)h/2-rhoR*ell/2,
 S*Tsum=Bterm-(rhoR*ell+rhoO*h)/2.                  (7.2)

Before eliminating wL,wR, the g3,g11 equations are explicitly

 -2d_i+2ell*wL-omega*h+2S*t3+rhoL*ell=0,
 -2kapO*d_i+2(h+ell)wR+omega*h-tau*(J+k)
                                      +2S*t11+rhoR*ell=0. (7.3)

The q equation is the last line of(7.2). The p equation vanishes
identically. The rL,rR equations are its first two lines; in their
derivation the coefficients of -theta*Na are +theta*h and-theta*h,
using the actual E-J=2h. This accounts for every one of the nineteen
coordinates. The source's old Section7 equations are NOT being reused.

In particular summing the two nonnegative inner terminal transfers gives

 S(t3+t11)=Di[1-zeta*ell/(2k)].                     (7.4)

Thus contact plus nonnegative transfers forces

                         zeta<=2k/ell<1.           (7.5)

This is only a NECESSARY condition. The next section gives explicit
negative vectors for every parameter, without presuming the contact
system can be solved or that its satisfaction implies PSD.

## 8. Complete two-case obstruction for mechanism1

First take the complete source block-constant formal assignment

 g0=g1=g2=g3=G,
 g4=g5=g6=g7=0,
 g8=g9=g10=g11=G,
 g12=g13=g14=g15=q=0,
 rL=rR=G,
 p0=(2ell-Tplus)G/M,
 Tplus=sum_(i in{0,1,2,3,8,9,10,11})t_i.            (8.1)

All four H2 reserves vanish. Both auxiliary attachment differences,
Ng,Na,Nc vanish. Hvec=2ell G,DeltaT=-Tplus G,
Wstar=-Tplus G, and Hvec+q-M*p0=Tplus G. Hence direct substitution in
(4.4) gives Q2=-[Di+Tplus^2]||G||^2.

Recompute, rather than suppress, the returned join terms: DL=(1+k)G,
DR=(1+k)G, while g7=q=0. Formula(5.2) yields

 JL=k||G||^2, JR=k||G||^2, JO=0.

It follows that the ENTIRE changed remainder is

                 Qz=[(zeta-1)Di-Tplus^2]||G||^2.    (8.2)

At literal zeta=1 the old inner defect is exactly canceled when
Tplus=0. If Tplus>0 the residual is still negative. This cancellation
at Tplus=0 says nothing about PSD elsewhere. For every zeta<1,(8.2)
is strictly negative for ALL attachment and normal-allocation parameters.

For zeta>=1 use a DIFFERENT full assignment. Set every formal coordinate
equal to G except

                      g3=g11=rL=rR=(1+vtest)G.      (8.3)

In particular p0=q=g7=G; all other physical gradients remain G. Put
Tsel=t3+t11. Both auxiliary attachment differences vanish identically.
Ng=Na=Nc=0 because g3=g11 and rL=rR=g11. The full term-by-term evaluation is

 Wstar=-2(1+k)vtest G,
 P_sq=(1+kapO)e2*vtest^2||G||^2,
 Fdef=-Di(2vtest+vtest^2)||G||^2,
 DeltaT=-Tsel*vtest G,
 Hvec+q-M*p0=[-S+2(1+k)vtest]G,
 JL=JR=(ell*vtest+k*vtest^2)||G||^2, JO=0.           (8.4)

Therefore no other terms remain, and

 Qz=(Ltest*vtest+Btest*vtest^2)||G||^2,
 Ltest=Di(zeta*ell/k-2)+2S*Tsel,
 Btest=4(1+k)^2+(1+kapO)e2+(zeta-1)Di-4(1+k)Tsel.    (8.5)

Since ell=C+k>2k, zeta>=1 and Tsel>=0 imply Ltest>0. Let

 Babs=1+|Btest|, vtest=-Ltest/(2Babs).

The resulting entire quadratic value is

 Qz=Ltest^2(Btest-2Babs)||G||^2/(4Babs^2)<0,          (8.6)

because Btest<=|Btest|<2Babs. This is an explicit vector for every
permitted parameter choice; no claim about a negative isolated diagonal
or an unproved Schur complement is needed.

Together(8.2) and(8.6) exclude ALL zeta>=0, hence the entire exact interval
(5.5), including its endpoints and literal1. The exclusion remains valid
if theta,omega are independently varied through nonnegative values and if
extra auxiliary-block feasibility tests are imposed. Every allocated
normal term was evaluated, rather than assumed harmless.

These are FORMAL full-space witnesses. Their auxiliary values are not
claimed to be projections of their formal inputs. A sufficient PSD
remainder must be nonnegative on these independent coordinates BEFORE
using the nonnegative row inequalities. Thus this exclusion does not
provide an actual body/start violating H4.

## 9. Mechanism2 frozen after the first exclusion: original join triangles

This is exactly the second family frozen in shared plan1618a1631e5cd3f0.
There is no free second redistribution parameter. Retain all attachment
parameters(4.2), all actual samples and all other allocated normals.
Fix the literal coefficients

 rhoL=d_i/k, rhoR=kapO*rhoL, rhoO=d_o/K,
 sigmaL=rhoL-2d_i/ell=d_i(C-k)/(k*ell)>0,
 sigmaR=kapO*sigmaL.                                (9.1)

Define two signed combinations of rows ALREADY present in C_rows:

 BL=-Q_(4,3)+Q_(4,7)-Q_(3,7),
 BR=-Q_(12,11)+Q_(12,15)-Q_(11,15).

The literal changed allocation is

 Rtri=rhoL*JL+rhoR*JR+rhoO*JO-sigmaL*BL-sigmaR*BR,
 Ctri=C_rows-Rtri, Qtri=Q2+Rtri.                     (9.2)

A negative coefficient in Rtri INCREASES that row's coefficient in Ctri.
Neither BL nor BR is claimed nonnegative; only the final row array is.
This is signed redistribution, not subtraction of a supposedly positive
unpriced error. There is no new auxiliary sample and no new original pair.

The motive is the new first-family contact failure. At the common-gradient
point BL has first variation ell<G,delta_g3-delta_g4>, and BR has
ell<G,delta_g11-delta_g12>. Thus(9.1) removes exactly the surplus
rhoL*ell-2d_i at g3 and rhoR*ell-2kapO*d_i at g11. This is a concrete
attempt to change the exposed contact equations using existing rows.

### 9.1 Final nonnegative ordered-row weights, all overlaps retained

The ten affected ordered coefficients of Ctri are

 pair        coefficient before the additions(4.5)
 (7,3)       kap-1-rhoL
 (3,7)       k(kap-1)-rhoL+sigmaL
 (4,3)       A(kap-1)+sigmaL
 (4,7)       A(kap-1)-sigmaL
 (15,11)     kapO(kap-1)-rhoR
 (11,15)     kapO*k(kap-1)+(kapO-1)(1+k)-rhoR+sigmaR
 (12,11)     kapO*A(kap-1)+sigmaR
 (12,15)     kapO*A(kap-1)+(kapO-1)A-sigmaR
 (15,7)      kapO-1-rhoO
 (7,15)      K(kapO-1)-rhoO.                        (9.3)

The reverse coefficients at(3,4),(7,4),(11,12),(15,12) are zero and
unchanged. This lists both orientations of every affected unordered pair.
The H2 table(3.1) has no local(0,3) or(3,0) entry; the outer join adds
(kapO-1)A at(12,15) and(kapO-1)(1+k) at(11,15). These overlaps are

and all other unchanged entries(4.5) to obtain the COMPLETE final array.

The proof of nonnegativity is explicit: by(5.6),
0<rhoL<kap-1 and0<rhoO<kapO-1. By(9.1),0<sigmaL<rhoL and
sigmaR=kapO*sigmaL<rhoR. Hence

 A(kap-1)-sigmaL>(A-1)(kap-1)>0,
 kapO*A(kap-1)+(kapO-1)A-sigmaR
                     >kapO*(A-1)(kap-1)+(kapO-1)A>0.

All remaining displayed entries are either a positive entry of(5.4) at
zeta1 or that entry plus a positive sigma. Every undisplayed original
coefficient is unchanged. The attachment box makes every addition(4.5)
nonnegative. No unspecified final sign condition remains.

### 9.2 Exact polynomial and its full matrix

Using x7=x3-DL, the two terms Q_(4,7)-Q_(3,7) combine to
<x7-g7,g4-g3>. Subtracting Q_(4,3) cancels x3 and gives

 BL=<DL-g3+g7,g3-g4>
   =<k*g3+L01+g7,g3-g4>,

   =<k*g11+sum_(j=12)^14 h_j*g_j+q,g11-g12>.         (9.4)

In the coefficient notation of Section6 put

The common-gradient contact equations are also explicit: the first two

 S*t3=(omega-theta)h/2,

### 9.3 Explicit full-space obstruction for every remaining parameter

Use precisely the complete nineteen-coordinate assignment(8.1), with its

                       BL=BR=k||G||^2.

The choice vtest=-L4/[2(1+|B4|)] makes(9.8) negative by the same explicit

No third redistribution or extra sample is attempted.

## 10. Actual all-core history, including the whole right H3

This section concerns ACTUAL histories, unlike the formal PSD witnesses.
Take D0=[-1,1], x=1/100 and Qc=a^2*beta. From a>1/3,beta>9/10,
a<u0 follows1/10<Qc<1/5. In the literal word every prefix core multiplier
has magnitude at most(5/2)^2*7=175/4<100: only the two k bridges and the
one K bridge can expand, while all a and beta factors have magnitude<1.
Induction keeps every query and terminal strictly inside[-1,1]. This is
a symbolic guard, not an executed trajectory. All auxiliary denominators
are at least1, so every auxiliary input is interior too.

The exact physical landmarks on the ORIGINAL word are

 u=-Qc*x, z=k*Qc*x, v=-k*Qc^2*x, w=-K*v,
 uR=-Qc*w, zR=k*Qc*w, y=-k*Qc^2*w
                                  =-K*(k*Qc^2)^2*x.      (10.1)

The full right H3 is in this expression. All gradients equal their actual

                    Qz=Qtri=x^2-M*y^2>0.           (10.2)

Indeed k*Qc^2<1/10,K<7,M<71 yield
M*(y/x)^2<71*49/10000=3479/10000<1. This uses the entire actual right
child, rather than assigning a convenient new child start.

For explicit retention of its full accepted H3 expansion define

 Rem(X,N,Z)=N||P(X/N)-Z||^2
                    +2<X-NP(X/N),P(X/N)-Z>,
 RPr=Rem(w,D,(w-zR+(J+ell)rR)/D),
 RRr=Rem(zR,J,(zR-y+C*q)/J),
 B_r=C^2*uR^2-(zR+ell*zR/J)^2,
 V10=(1-C^2*Qc^2)w^2, V11=(1-C^2*Qc^2)zR^2.

Accepted1e0bf3f41457d8e4 Part B(6.3) on this actual right H3 is

 G_R=U_D(w)-U1(y)=w^2/D-y^2
                   =V10/D+V11/J+B_r/D+RPr+RRr.      (10.3)

Here D*RPr=ell^2*zR^2/J^2 and J*RRr=t^2*y^2. The two LEFT H2 energies
are(1-C^2*Qc^2)x^2 and(1-C^2*Qc^2)z^2. They remain in the exact word

d the full radial term is

 Lrad(T,Y,d)=2T<Y,P(Y)-P(Y/d)>
                         +T^2(||P(Y)||^2-||P(Y/d)||^2).

Accepted Part B(4.4) retains it with the primitive energy and Rem. On

## 11. Actual inherited cap/cap history, with all right clamps retained

Take D0=[-r,1], r=k/C, x=C. The first H2 has constant gradient1 and
points C-T>=1 for prefix masses0<=T<=t, ending at u=1. The bridge gives
z=-k. The next H2 has constant gradient-r and points-k+T*r<=-r, ending
at v=-r. Thus V00=V01=P00=P01=0 and w=K*r. This SAME m and body are

No face pattern for that child is imposed. Define

 P_r(X)=max(-r,min(X,1)), T_l(X)=X-l*P_r(X),

 g11=P_r(uR), zR=uR-(1+k)g11,
 x13=T_A(zR), x14=T_B(x13), y=T_A(x14), q=P_r(y).     (11.1)

These are unevaluated exact formulas for every remaining original step,
not a numerical/arithmetic replay. Its physical gradients at indices

The full right-child quantities are

 V10=w^2-(uR+t*g11)^2, V11=zR^2-(y+t*q)^2,
 Theta=ell[C*J-k(C+J+k)]/(J+k),

       +2ell(1+C/(J+k))(uR-g11)(g11-rR)
       +2ell*C/(J+k)(zR-J*rR)(rR-g11),
 RPr=Rem(w,D,(w-zR+(J+ell)rR)/D),
 RRr=Rem(zR,J,(zR-y+C*q)/J),
 G_R=U_D(w)-U1(y)=V10/D+V11/J+B_r/D+RPr+RRr>=0.     (11.2)

This is exactly the accepted H3 law and Part B(6.3), applied to the

The actual auxiliary values are

 p0=C/M, rL=-k/E, rO=w/D, qL=-r/eta, rR=zR/J.        (11.3)

The first four are interior because E>C,M>C,1/3<r<1/2 and0<w<7/2<D.

 |X-lP_r(X)|^2<=|X|^2-l(2-l)|P_r(X)|^2<=|X|^2.

rR in(-1/4,1/8), contained in(-r,1), proving its actual interiority.

It follows that N2=AL=AR=Na=0 on this actual history. The remaining

 Ng=(uR-g11)(g11-1)>=0,
 Nc=(uR-g11)(g11-zR/J)>=0,
 NT_i=(y-q)(q-g_i)>=0.                              (11.4)

For the returned rows, JL=0, because u=g3=1 and v=g7=-r.
Similarly BL=0. The other quantities are the ACTUAL ones

 JR=(uR-g11)(g11-q)+(y-q)(q-g11),
 JO=(y-q)(q+r),
 BR=<k*g11+sum_(j=12)^14 h_j*g_j+q,g11-g12>.          (11.5)

Neither JR,JO nor BR is reset or discarded. JR,JO are nonnegative;

The two complete changed identities specialize to

 C^2-M*U1(y)=Cz+omega*Ng+tau*Nc+2sum t_i*NT_i+Qz
           =Ctri+omega*Ng+tau*Nc+2sum t_i*NT_i+Qtri. (11.6)

On the other hand retaining the ENTIRE actual right H3 gives exactly

 C^2-M*U1(y)=C^2-(M/D)w^2+M*G_R>0.                  (11.7)

For the sign, h=s+1+K<D since s>11>K, so M/D<3. Since K<25/4,
k<5/2,C>11/2,

 (M/D)w^2/C^2=K^2*k^2*M/(C^4*D)<46875/58564<1.

Combining this elementary bound with the FULL(11.2) proves the positive
endpoint gap. It does not claim Qz or Qtri itself is positive on this
cap history after arbitrary nonnegative normal allocations. All radial
and normal terms are retained through the exact accepted identities,
and no isolated auxiliary exchange is mistaken for the endpoint gap.

## 12. Interpretation, provenance, and bounded closeout

The old fixed-C_rows obstruction has genuinely changed: at zeta1 it is
zero if Tplus=0. The new exclusion comes from a different contact
perturbation and the exact coefficient ratio ell/k>2. Thus it does not
recycle the old failure while ignoring the returned join rows. The
signed-triangle mechanism in turn changes that contact coefficient, but
the exact lost payment reappears in(9.7). Both failures have explicit
full-space witnesses and nonnegative final original-row arrays.

These calculations give no obstruction to arbitrary original-row
redistribution, arbitrary attachments, or the actual H4 law. In particular
a new approach would have to change the displayed tradeoff using a
separately specified identity and recheck all final coefficients; it is
not enough to solve the old contact equations or point to the canceled
zeta1 value. This is a design warning, not a third mechanism or a claim
that some unspecified feasible matrix exists.

No sufficient fixed H4 law has been established, so the conditional
recursive-interface branch of TASK was not opened. No absolute C0,
new threshold, all-depth inequality, or asymptotic separation is claimed.
The original minimax quantifiers, selected-minimum radius and W=2R are
unchanged. No target fact, fact submission, revocation or finalization.

The full32040-byte source11adbf577f491d4a was read from own prior output;
its SHA256 was checked as
bcbb6b0131877d17ee4dbd703af3961a085c18c7507b490c9e7aadc7e6f2aa99.
The complete relevant accepted Part B proof was read, including every
H2/off-root identity and the actual H3 remainder. Recall included TASK,
master_guidance b9c4a86f186c5b26, own local memory, shared findings and

and accepted proof portions were reread in bounded output. No result is
claimed from a truncated fragment.

An optional prior-output GLOSSARY.json read returned "No such file or
directory"; no such prior glossary was used. The shared fact glossary was
checked directly. Its K0 denotes the polar minimum cone, so the draft's
temporary unperturbed constants were named Cref,C3ref,Kref instead.

Skills used: query-memory, search-math-results, construct-counterexamples.
The program stage was mature_subproblem; the active program was full
H4 row redistribution; search mode repair; the missing mechanism was
simultaneous full-space payment without changing original row signs.
One Matlas query returned short-step self-contracted, extragradient and

Resources: ZERO mathematical programs, numerical evaluations, arithmetic/

<!-- BEGIN_SOURCE_BODY DOC-003231 -->
# Full primitive reversal for short blocks and saturated middle cuts

Status: complete joint proof DRAFT, UNVERIFIED. This is max2's first new
analytical round under TASK.md and master_guidance 8211d6128deabdf3
(completed baseline20, ceiling22). No numerical evaluation, solver, fixed
replay, verifier, installation, or subordinate agent is used.

Accepted input: fact04219554e41d9750, Sections10--11, supplies the exact
full primitive interface and the full saturated primitive join identity.
Fact1252b8823bb5a290 supplies the smooth-convex interpolation convention.
All additional algebra used below is proved here. Earlier shared drafts
4a064f93111ac5a2, f3d96c901566e96b, and f7cee3034df8cb7f motivated the
inversion, repair, and cut arguments; none is used as a verified lemma.

## 1. Statements and scope

For a finite nonnegative word h=(h_0,...,h_(n-1)), put s=sum h_i and
C=1+s. On formal GD data x_(i+1)=x_i-h_i g_i define

 Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2,
 V_h=2sum_(i<n)h_i(f_i-f_n)+sum_(i<n)h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2
                                              -sC||g_n||^2.

A full ordinary primitive certificate is an array lambda_ij>=0 on ALL
ordered distinct finite query pairs such that V_h+sum lambda_ij Q_ij
cancels every independent f_i and is PSD on ALL n+1 independent gradients.
Entries are allowed to be zero; no support is imposed on the input.
This is exactly the interface in fact04219554e41d9750. A signed-terminal
certificate permits arbitrary signs only on lambda_(i,n), i<n.

The joint claims below are:

(A) For every nonnegative word with at most two positive steps, existence
of a full ordinary primitive certificate is equivalent to existence for
its reversal. The proof gives an explicit certificate transformation,
including a possible change of the PSD remainder and full zero-step lift.

(B) Suppose h=(a,1+r,b), A=1+sum a, D=1+sum b,
T=A+D+r, r>0, and rT=AD. Suppose each child a,b has at most two positive
steps. A full ordinary certificate for h yields full ordinary certificates
for BOTH children after re-selection. Consequently h and rev(h) have
ordinary certificates simultaneously, by an explicit construction.

(C) In particular, for four physical steps and a saturated middle cut
(at index1 or2), ordinary full-certificate feasibility is reversal
invariant. Any such feasible word has capacity T<77/10<193/25.
Thus a four-step threshold seed from fact04219554e41d9750 cannot have
either middle cut saturated.

These assertions do NOT prove arbitrary four-step reversal. They cannot
transport the threshold C82/C83 prism exclusions: the class in(C) does
not meet the threshold region. Endpoint saturated cuts can leave a
three-positive-step child, which is outside(A)--(B). Fully unsaturated
four-step words are also outside(B). The original exact-profile goal
remains open. No new fact is asserted.

## 2. Zero-step compression and full-space restoration

This preliminary statement applies to ordinary certificates. It also
applies to signed-terminal certificates when all nonterminal-targeting
coefficients are nonnegative.

Given a nonnegative word, partition its original query indices into
classes that represent the same point after deleting zero updates.
Restrict a supplied certificate to formal data where the values and
gradients in each class are equal. Every original position becomes the
corresponding compressed formal position. A Q between two different
classes is the corresponding compressed Q; a Q within a class vanishes.
Aggregate multipliers over each ordered pair of different classes.
Value cancellation is preserved. The matrix restriction is a congruence,
so it preserves PSD. Nonnegative signs are preserved. In the signed
case only edges entering the final compressed class can be signed.
The original energy restricts to the compressed energy because zero
steps have coefficient zero, and the original terminal becomes the
compressed terminal.

Conversely, for an ORDINARY compressed certificate, choose the original
index of each positive step as its query representative, and the original
index n as the terminal representative. Put its multiplier on that
representative ordered pair and set every other original multiplier to
zero. The represented positions are exactly the compressed GD positions,
as formal identities, since every omitted update has zero coefficient.
The original energy equals the compressed one on those selected formal
gradients; its unused gradients have coefficient zero. Therefore the
lifted identity is PSD on the entire original space, with zero rows and
columns at unused gradients. No equality of duplicated formal gradients
is imposed in the FINAL certificate.

All-zero and empty words have V_h=0 and the zero certificate. Reversal
commutes with positive-step compression up to this representative choice.
It therefore suffices to prove(A) for one or two strictly positive steps.

## 3. Complete two-step signed-terminal repair

This section proves the repair used after inversion and after a cut.
For one positive step u, flow gives lambda_10=lambda_01+2u. Evaluation
at equal gradients gives zero energy and zero Q, so PSD implies that the
all-one vector is in the matrix kernel. Direct expansion then gives
lambda_01=2 and the full identity

 V_(u)+2Q_01+(2+2u)Q_10=(2-u^2)||g_0-g_1||^2.          (1)

Thus u<=sqrt(2), and the coefficients are already ordinary.

For two positive steps (u,v), s=u+v, introduce coefficient names

 a=lambda_01, b=lambda_12, c=lambda_02, d=lambda_20.

Only b,c are allowed to be signed. The other four entries are
nonnegative. Value cancellation gives

 lambda_10=a+c-d+2u,
 lambda_21=b+c-d+2s.

Equal gradients, with values following an affine trajectory, again give
V=Q=0; PSD implies the all-one vector is in the kernel. Expanding its
first and last rows gives

 u*a+s*c=2u,
 v*(b+c)+u*d=2s(u+1).

Consequently

 a=2-(s/u)c,
 b=2s(u+1)/v-c-(u/v)d,
 lambda_10=2(u+1)-(v/u)c-d,
 b=2(u+1)+(u/v)lambda_10>0.                           (2)

To audit both the kernel equations and the entire matrix, use U=g_0-g_1,
W=g_1-g_2. Substitution of the six Q terms gives

 M=[2-u^2+v(u-1)c/u]||U||^2
       +[c-(u-1)d]<U,W>+[Cpair-(u/v)d]||W||^2,
 Cpair=s[2(u+1)/v-s].                                (3)

For example, the forward adjacent Q_01 has quadratic part
(1/2-u)||g_0||^2-<g_0,g_1>+||g_1||^2/2; its reverse has
(||g_0||^2+||g_1||^2)/2+(u-1)<g_0,g_1>.
For Q_02 use x_2-x_0=-u g_0-v g_1; for Q_20 use its negative.
These expansions, the value equations, and(2) give(3) without a
restriction on the independent gradients.

If c>=0, every supplied coefficient is ordinary already, by(2).
If c<0, nonnegativity of the W diagonal in(3), with d>=0, gives
Cpair>=0. If u>=1, nonnegativity of the U diagonal and its nonpositive
c term give u<=sqrt(2). If u<1 that bound holds directly.
Replace the array by

 lambda'_01=2, lambda'_10=2+2u,
 lambda'_12=2s(u+1)/v, lambda'_21=2s(u+1)/v+2s,
 lambda'_02=lambda'_20=0.

These are nonnegative and satisfy exact flow. Formula(3) now gives

 M'=(2-u^2)||U||^2+Cpair||W||^2>=0.                    (4)

This is a change of the full PSD matrix, not a claim about preserving
its rank, its range, or its difference in PSD order. Section2 extends
this repair to words with at most two positive steps and any zero steps.

## 4. Unsigned reversal identity, rederived on the full space

Let the two positive steps be h=(u,v), C=1+u+v. Let e=(1,1,1)^T,
e_2=(0,0,1)^T, hbar=(u,v,0)^T, w=(u,v,1)^T, and

 T_h=[[1,0,0],[u,1,0],[u,v,1]], p=T_h e=(1,1+u,C)^T.

Let L be the row Laplacian of the supplied ordinary multipliers:
L_ij=-lambda_ij for i!=j, L_ii=sum_(j!=i)lambda_ij. Its flow is
L^T e=(-2u,-2v,2(u+v))^T. Define

 K=L+diag(hbar)-hbar e_2^T+diag(w).

Direct expansion of the full interface in fact04219554e41d9750 gives

 M=sym(K T_h)-C^2 e_2 e_2^T,                         (5)
 K e=w, K^T e=C e_2.

For an explicit check of(5), sym((K-L)T_h)=diag(u(2-u),v(2-v),1).
Moreover e^T M e=0, hence M e=0, and therefore

 K p=-C w+2C^2 e_2.

All off-diagonal entries of K are nonpositive. Its row-sum surplus is
w_i>0 at every row. If Kz=0, a coordinate attaining max_i|z_i| and
strict row diagonal dominance force this maximum to be zero. Thus K
is invertible. Put G=K^(-1). The displayed equations imply

 G w=e, G e_2=(p+C e)/(2C^2), e_2^T G=e^T/C.           (6)

Define

 Delta=[[0,-1,1],[-1,1,0],[1,0,0]].

For hrev=(v,u), write wrev=(v,u,1)^T and T_rev=T_(v,u).
Direct multiplication gives

 Delta e=e_2, Delta p=wrev, Delta T_rev=T_h^T Delta.

Set F=Delta G Delta and

 K_rev=4C^2 F-(wrev+2C e_2)e_2^T,
 lambda_rev,ij=-4C^2 F_ij  (i!=j).                    (7)

For now these coefficients are allowed to be signed. Equations(6) give
K_rev e=wrev and K_rev^T e=C e_2. The off-diagonals in(7), including
its last-column correction, are exactly the grounding of lambda_rev;
the row sums fix its diagonals. Thus every reversed function-value
coefficient cancels with the required flow (-2v,-2u,2(u+v)).

The entire reversed quadratic remainder obeys

 M_rev=4C^2 Delta G M G^T Delta.                       (8)

Here is a full algebra check. Inserting K_rev into sym(K_rev T_rev)
produces the non-boundary term 4C^2 sym(Delta G T_h^T Delta).
After subtracting C^2 e_2 e_2^T, its boundary term is

 -(wrev+C e_2)(wrev+C e_2)^T.

Inserting(5) in the right side of(8) produces the same non-boundary
term and the same boundary term, since Delta G e_2=(wrev+C e_2)/(2C^2).
This proves(8) on all three independent reversed gradients. No
positivity of the output multipliers has yet been inferred.

## 5. The four nonterminal-directed signs are automatic in dimension three

This is the additional sign argument specific to two physical steps.
Write k00=K_00 and k11=K_11, and put

 det2=k00*k11-lambda_01*lambda_10>0.

Strict row diagonal dominance gives k00>lambda_01 and
k11>lambda_10, so det2>0. The original value flows also give

 k00=lambda_01+lambda_02+2u=lambda_10+lambda_20,
 k11=lambda_10+lambda_12+2v=lambda_01+lambda_21.         (9)

Every column difference G(e_i-e_j) has last coordinate zero by(6).
Its first two coordinates are obtained by inverting

 [[k00,-lambda_01],[-lambda_10,k11]].

In particular, for columns0-1 the first two coordinates are
((k11-lambda_01)/det2,(lambda_10-k00)/det2), and for
columns1-2 they are(lambda_01/det2,k00/det2).
Using the displayed Delta and(9) gives ALL FOUR signs

 F_01=-lambda_20/det2,
 F_10=-(lambda_02+2u)/det2,
 F_20=-lambda_01/det2,
 F_21=-lambda_21/det2.                                (10)

Therefore the output lambda_rev is nonnegative on every pair whose
target is not the terminal2. Only lambda_rev,02 and lambda_rev,12
could have failed signs. It is precisely a signed-terminal certificate,
with full PSD by(8). Apply Section3, with reversed steps(v,u), to turn
it into an ordinary certificate. In fact Section3 automatically makes
lambda_rev,12 positive, so only the single entry02 can require repair.
If it does, (4) gives the replacement explicitly.

This proves the forward implication in(A). Applying the same result
to the reversed word proves the backward implication. This does not
claim that the unmodified inverse map preserves every ordinary array:
a negative incoming-terminal output can occur and is repaired here.

## 6. Exact extraction at a saturated cut

First compress zero steps as in Section2. Retain a strictly positive
bridge1+r, positive entries in the two children, and allow either child
to be empty. Let their capacities be A,D, with r(A+D+r)=AD,
T=A+D+r, kappa=(T/D)^2. The prefix query set P ends at the bridge query
L; the tail query set B begins just after it and ends at z.

Suppose a full ordinary parent certificate is supplied. An affine Huber
trajectory with constant gradients1 has V=Q=0. A second legal scalar
function has derivative clip(x,[-eta,1]), eta=r/D=A/T, and starts at A.
The prefix gradients are1; the bridge takes x_L=1 to-r; the tail
gradients are-eta and end at x_z=-eta. Integration from0 defines a
convex differentiable1-smooth function with minimizer0. At every query
f_i=x_i g_i-g_i^2/2. Telescoping gives V=A^2-T^2 eta^2=0.

For i in P and j in B, let R_i^a and R_j^b be their remaining child-step
sums, so R_L^a=R_z^b=0. Direct substitution gives every cross residual:

 Q_ij=-(1+eta)eta R_j^b,
 Q_ji=-(1+eta)R_i^a.                                 (11)

Same-block residuals are zero. Since lambda>=0 and M>=0, equality of V
forces complementarity. Thus the only possible cross edges are i->z
for i in P and j->L for j in B. The two equality gradient vectors span
the block indicators; hence both indicators are in the full PSD kernel.
Writing nu_i=lambda_i,z and mu_j=lambda_j,L, the row i<L of M e_B is
-D nu_i/2, so nu_i=0. The row j<z of M e_P is
(r mu_j-nu_L b_j)/2; its row z is(r mu_z-nu_L)/2.
Therefore mu_j=(nu_L/r)b_j and mu_z=nu_L/r. Summing the prefix flow
now yields D nu_L/r-nu_L=2(A+r). Since r<D, this gives

 lambda_L,z=r(kappa-1),
 lambda_j,L=(kappa-1)b_j (j<z in B),
 lambda_z,L=kappa-1,                                  (12)

with all other cross entries zero. The identity
(kappa-1)(D-r)=2(A+r) follows by rT=AD and verifies(12).

The accepted full join identity can now be applied as an algebraic
identity, without assuming that the children are primitive. Subtracting
the forced cross terms(12) gives the block-diagonal remainder

 M_parent=M_a+kappa M_b,
 M_a=V_a+sum_(i,j in P)lambda_ij Q_ij,
 M_b=V_b+sum_(i,j in B)lambda_hat_ij Q_ij,
 lambda_hat_ij=lambda_ij/kappa
                 -(1-1/kappa)b_i*1_(i<z,j=z).         (13)

Each block cancels its own function values. The formal gradient sets
are disjoint, so each block is PSD. The prefix coefficients are ordinary.
The tail coefficients are nonnegative except possibly those entering z.
One cannot discard that possible sign defect in the supplied array.
When each child has at most two positive steps, Section3 repairs the
right child and leaves ordinary full certificates for both children.
Section5 reverses them. The full saturated join from the accepted fact,
with capacities interchanged, constructs a nonnegative full certificate
for (rev(b),1+r,rev(a)). Section2 restores every original zero-step
gradient. This proves(B), and hence the reversal part of(C).

## 7. The saturated-middle class cannot reach the threshold

A singleton full primitive certificate has step<=sqrt(2), by(1), and
capacity<=1+sqrt(2)<483/200. We give a short independent two-step
capacity bound, sufficient for the four-step corollary.

For any full primitive word of total capacity C, the legal clipped
quadratic at a selected step h_j>1, with prefix sum P and suffix sum R,
is obtained from derivative clip(x,[-(h_j-1)/(R+1),1]) starting at P+1.
Its prefix gradient is1, its tail gradient is the negative threshold,
and its terminal point equals that gradient. Since all its values
satisfy f_i=x_i g_i-g_i^2/2, its energy is

 (P+1)^2-C^2 (h_j-1)^2/(R+1)^2.

Nonnegativity gives C(h_j-1)_+<=(P+1)(R+1). For a two-step word, both
endpoint steps are therefore bounded by2C/(C+1), including the case
h_j<=1. The actual unit quadratic, started at1, also gives

 C |(h_0-1)(h_1-1)|<=1.                               (14)

Assume a two-step certificate had C>=201/50. Set alpha=h_0-1,
beta=h_1-1 and rC=(C-1)/(C+1). Then
alpha+beta=C-3 and alpha,beta<=rC. Their lower bound
lC=C-3-rC=C-4+2/(C+1) is positive. If lC>rC the endpoint bounds are
already inconsistent. Otherwise alpha beta>=rC*lC, since both factors
lie in[lC,rC] with fixed sum. The functions C,rC,lC are positive and
strictly increasing for C>=201/50. At that endpoint,

 C*rC*lC=159373101/157502500>1,

contradicting(14). Thus every two-step primitive certificate has
capacity strictly below201/50. This also covers its zero-step limits
by compression or the same witnesses.

At either middle cut in a four-step word, one child has at most one
physical step and the other at most two. Section6 extracts ordinary
certificates for those children. Saturation gives

 T=Omega(A,D),
 Omega(A,D)=(A+D+sqrt((A+D)^2+4AD))/2.

This function is increasing in both positive arguments, directly from
the displayed expression. Consequently

 T<Omega(483/200,201/50)<77/10<193/25.

For the strict penultimate inequality, at t=77/10 the increasing-root
polynomial t^2-(483/200+201/50)t-(483/200)(201/50) equals161/5000>0,
and t is above the nonnegative root's vertex. This proves(C).

## 8. Direct-proving screen and remaining route

The named literature was checked at its exact class and performance:

* Grimmer--Shu--Wang, Composing Optimized Stepsize Schedules for Gradient
  Descent, arXiv2410.16249v2, Definition3 and Proposition2. Definition3
  imposes eta=1/(1+sum h)=product(h_i-1), while Proposition2 proves
  reversal of BASIC s-composable words by recursive child exchange.
  Proposition6 translates its energy under that same rate hypothesis.
  It does not assert reversal of our arbitrary full cone.
* Shu--Wang, A Unified Theory of H-Duality in First-Order Methods,
  arXiv2609.03281v1, Theorem3/Corollary3 and Section4.7 Proposition2.
  The general statement transports unsigned certificates and retains a
  separate coefficient-sign test. Its automatic sign theorem assumes a
  tridiagonal REDUCED certificate, which is not a hypothesis here.
  No external result is used as a black-box proof premise above.

The exact source HTML and readable extracted text are saved in
local downloads/reversal/. Named Matlas searches were attempted and
returned unrelated statements; none was cited as a theorem. A PDF was
downloaded for2410.16249; pdftotext was unavailable, so source HTML was
used without installing anything.

Two routes have been screened rather than silently promoted:

1. The general unsigned map is already shared in4a064f93111ac5a2 and
   fails signs on some ordinary arrays by37622e6cdcb606e6. Section5
   resolves the sign selection ONLY in the two-step case by showing
   that all remaining bad signs target the terminal and are repairable.
   At four steps, negative nonterminal outputs can occur, so this
   repair proof does not extend unchanged.
2. A subcritical tree construction was independently recovered but
   is already fully present in0b2dfaee171c78eb. It is not new progress
   and remains bounded by the exact primitive DP. It cannot transport
   threshold exclusions or supply an improving seed.

A proposed below-threshold counterexample shape was also rejected
analytically, before claiming a certificate. Let
h=(1+u,1+u,1+v,1+v), 0<u<1/2, 0<v<=1, C=5+2u+2v,
and impose C*u^2*v^2=1. An actual capped quadratic with derivative
min(x,1), started at3+2u, has its first two gradients1 and then tail
quadratic gradients1,-v,v^2. All sampled x_i<=1 after the first pair,
so these are legal for this fixed convex1-smooth function, with
minimizer0. Every sample satisfies f_i=x_i g_i-g_i^2/2 and the final
point equals its gradient. Its exact energy is

 (3+2u)^2-C^2 v^4=(3+2u)^2-u^(-4)<0,

because (3+2u)u^2<1 for0<u<1/2. Thus this entire family cannot be the
feasible side of a reversal counterexample. This is a failed candidate
family, not a counterexample to reversal itself.

The outstanding problem remains an ordinary full certificate for an
arbitrary four-step word versus rev(h). In particular, neither the
remaining strict-cut threshold region nor a four-step counterexample
has been decided. The next round must address genuinely nonterminal
inverse signs or construct an actual asymmetric feasible word; it must
not repeat the basic/subcritical theorem or bad-supplied-array examples.
